100b3(phal chen): ba'i tsong ban zhes bya ba bsnyen bkur te//rigs kyi bu 'di ltar kho mos de bzhin gshegs pa bcu po de dag la sogs pa bskal pa bzang po 'od zer bzang po de la skye bo'i rigs sna tshogs su skyes nas 
100b4(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa bye ba phrag drug cu bsnyen bkur te/kho mos de bzhin gshegs ba de dag thams cad la mchod pa byas so//de bzhin gshegs pa re re'i spyan sngar 'gro ba'i tshe 'ang sems can 
100b5(phal chen): gyi khams mtha' dang dbus med pa/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongs su smin par byas so//de bzhin gsheg sa ba re re'i spyan sngar 'gro pa'i tshe 'ang ting nge 'dzin gyi 
100b6(phal chen): sgo sna tshogs rab tu thob par gyur to/gzungs kyi sgo sna tshogs dang /mngon par bsgrub pa'i tshul sna tshogs dang /so so yang dag par rig pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i tshul na tshogs 
100b7(phal chen): dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi tshul rjes su rtogs par bya ba sna tshogs dang /chos snang ba'i sna thob par bya ba'i tshul sna tshogs dang /ye shes kyi tshul khong du chud par byed pa sna tshogs dang /
100b8(phal chen): phyogs rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i snang ba sna tshogs dang /zhing rgya mtsho sna tshogs la 'jam pa nyid khong du chad par bya ba'i snang ba sna tshogs dang de bzhin gshegs pa rgya mtsho mthong bar bya ba 
101a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par dmigs pa'i snang ba sna tshogs kyang rab tu thob po//yongs su gnon to//rnam par dag go/yongs su rdzogs so//yongs su gnas par gyur to//
101a2(phal chen): skyes so//byung ngo rnam par 'phel to//rab tu byung ngo//rnam par byung ngo//bskal pa 'od zer bzang po de la de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ji ltar rim gror byas pa de bzhin du bskal pa 
101a3(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad kyi/rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du/de bzhin gshegs pa snyed cig byung bar gyur pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa ji snyed cig 'jig rten gyi 
101a4(phal chen): khams/gzhan nas de dag tu byon te/chos bstan par gyur pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad las kyang chos bstan pa thos te/thos nas kyang yongs su bzung ngo//
101a5(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa de dagthams cad kyang kho mos bsnyen bkur to//rim gror byas so//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag gis bstan pa yang kho mos yongs su bzung ngo/
101a6(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa de de dag thams cad las kya nga kho mos byang chub sems pa'i rnam par thar pa sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya bas bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 
101a7(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba la yang dag par bskul ba 'di thob par bya ba'i tshul sna tshogs dang /rnam par thar pa'i tshul gyi sgo sna tshogs kyis thob par gyur to//de nas mtshan mo'i lha mo 'gro 
101a8(phal chen): ba thams cad bsrung ba'i smon lam la brtson pa'i 'od kyis/de'i tshe rnam par thar pa'i tshul 'di nyid yongs su brjod pa dang /yongs su bstan pa'i phyir tshe dpon gyi bu nor bzangs 
101b1(phal chen): la tshigs su bcad de smras pa/bsam gyis mi khyab rnam thar dam pa 'di//grogs po khyod kyis bdag la gang dris pa//bde bar gshegs pa'i byin gyis de bstan gyis//ma lus yang 
101b2(phal chen): dag ji bzhin bdag las nyon//bskal pa bsam yas yangs pa mtha' yas pa//rgya mtsho'i zhing gi rdul dang grangs mnyam pa//de kun 'das pa'i snga rol mdzad sa dga' ba'i//'jig 
101b3(phal chen): rten khams shig byung ba rin chen 'ong //de tshe bskal pa bzang po'i 'od kyang byung //de la rgyal ba dpag tu med pa skyes//thub pa'i dbang po de dag kho mo yis//rnam thar 'di la sgom zhing bsnyen 
101b4(phal chen): bkur to//rgyal po'i gnas rab yangs shing bzang byung ba//de'i ming ni dga' bas brgyan pa ste//de tshe sems can rnam par ma dag sems//mi bzang ngan du spyod pa der ni byung //der ni rgyal po rgyal 
101b5(phal chen): ba'i 'od byung ste//sems can de dag chos kyis bskyang ba byas/de'i bu ni rnam par rgyal ba ste//mdzes shing mtshan gyis yan lag brgyan pa'o//g.yon ldan stong phrag mang po dag kyang //de tshe 
101b6(phal chen): rgyal pos gsang bar byas mthong nas//gzhon nu snying brtse mi dga' kun nas 'khor/myur du dgye bar rgyal po de la gsol/rgyal pos thos de de tshe blon po rnams//ma lus kun bsdus de la 
101b7(phal chen): bka' stsal nas//kun gyis da la phyag byas phyir gsol ba//de ni rgyal po khyod grongs thabs gcig lags/rgyal po de ltar log par thugs bcug nas//sras de gsang bar de tshe byas 
101b8(phal chen): gyur kyang //bdag srog phangs par ma brtsis ngom zung med//rgyal po'i sras kyis gsad par byas ma btang //gsang bar byas pa de'i mas thos nas//rgyal po de la 'khor bcas gsol 
102a1(phal chen): @#//ba 'debs//dgung zla phyed cig sbyin pa ma lus par//'jig rten stsol du gzhon nu btang bar gtod//rgyal po des kyang de ltar gnang gyur nas//des ni ci 'dod sbyin pa yongs 
102a2(phal chen): btang //zla ba phyed du nyin mtshan sbyin byas gnas//nyi ma'i tha mar tshogs par gyur pa yi//zhags pa 'thub pa gang rnams ci 'dod pa//de la de byin 'chi bar sta gon gnas//rgyal po'i 
102a3(phal chen): gnas der 'gro ba mang po dag/tshong re du zhing kun kyang 'dus par gyur//byang chub shing drung bde gshegs de'i tshe//dam chos dbyangs mchog sgron ma'i rgyal bzhugs pas//'gro ba'i dbang po 
102a4(phal chen): smin par thugs chud nas//mchod sbyin ra bar thugs rje chen pos byon//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam 'phrul rnam phye bas//de'i khod du bde gshegs byon gyur nas//chos kyi sgron ma'i sprin gyi 
102a5(phal chen): sgra sgrogs pa'i//mdo sde'i rgyal po bde bar gshegs pas bshad//'gro ba mtha' dang dbus med rnam btul nas//de tshe byang chub tu yang lung yang bstan//rgyal po'i sras po rnam par rgyal 
102a6(phal chen): ba yang //shin tu dga' bas byang chub mchog tu zhugs//bde gshegs bsnyen bkur mchod pa rnam yangs pas//de la mchod byas dga' bar smras pa ni//'gro ba rnam 'dul skyabs su bdag 
102a7(phal chen): 'gyur ba gyi//mgon dang gnas dang dpung du'ang de bzhin zhes//rgyal po de'i zhal rang thar bar zhugs//yang dag byang du ba lam ni yongs tshol zhing //chos kyi rang bzhin rab tu brtag bya bas//der 
102a8(phal chen): gnas bskal pa brgya phrag mang du spyad//sdug bsngal rgya mtshor lhung zhing 'doms gyur pa'i//'gro ba mgon med kun la snying brtse bas//yang dag byang chub lam la yongs bsgoms nas//
102b1(phal chen): de tshe de'i rnam thar 'di thob po//bskal pa de la bde gshegs gang 'byung ba//de dag kun la'ang dga' bas bsnyen bkur nas//de dag kun la mchod pa chen po byas//chos kyi 'khor lo yongs 
102b2(phal chen): bskor bar gyur//de'i 'og tu bskal pa rgya mtsho rnams//zhing mang rgya mtsho rdul snyed kun la yang //rgyal ba ji snyed skyes par gyur pa dag/rab tu mchod de de dag bsnyen bkur byas//rnam par 
102b3(phal chen): rgyal zhes bya ba kho mo yin//nyam bde brtson rar gnas pa'i brtson mthong nas//de dag dgrol phyir lus kyang yongs su btang //rnam thar 'di yang de tshe thob par gyur//zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i 
102b4(phal chen): rdul phran rab bgrangs pa'i//bskal pa rgya mtsho mang por bsgom par byas//tshul mang mtha' dang dbus med mnyam med pas//skad cig re re dag tu rnam par spel//thub pa'i bdag po ji snyed 
102b5(phal chen): mthong gyur pa//de dag spyan sngar sngan chad 'di thob ste//sgo tshul rnam pa gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//de dag gis ni rnam thar 'di yang bstan//de dag rnam thar ma nor bsam las la//bskal 
102b6(phal chen): pa bye ba mang po kho mos bslabs//de la rgyal bas bkod cing grol gyur pa//de dag dam chos sprin las cig car 'thung //phyogs bcu kun gyi zhing rnams thams cad du//thogs pa med par lus 
102b7(phal chen): kyis rgyas par 'gengs//skad cig re rer zhing rgyud bsam yas pa'i//dus gsum rgyud rnams kun la'ang rab tu 'jug/dus gsum rgyal ba rgya mtsho ma lus pa//re re kun la'ang mngon du rab tu 
102b8(phal chen): lta//de dag lus kyi gzugs brnyan rgyal ba la//sprin du gyur pa rnams kyang spyan sngar 'grim//phyogs rnams kun tu nye bar phyin 'dod zhing //rgyal po'i spyan sngar 'dod pas shin tu bangos//
103a1(phal chen): @#//rgyan rnams ma lus mngon par char 'bebs shing //rgyal ba rnams la de dag mchod pa byed//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho che ba rnam yangs pa'i//zhu ba mtha' yas kun kyang zhu ba byed//
103a2(phal chen): rgyal ba'i mchod sprin mngon par char 'bebs pa//kun kyang ma lus yongs su 'dzin par byed//phyogs bcu ma lus kun tu de dag 'dod//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor kun gyi spyan sngar 'bebs//
103a3(phal chen): gzugs mang rnam pa sna tshogs bstan gnas shing //rnam 'phrul sna tshogs nye bar ston pa byed//kha dog mtha' yas rang gi lus gzugs kyis//phyogs rnams thams cad rnam pa stong du 
103a4(phal chen): 'gengs//de dag lus gcig las kyang gzugs kyi rnams//mtha' dang dbus med rnam par ston par byed//ba spu re re'i sgo nas grangs med pa'i//'od zer mang po rgya mtsho de dag 'gyed//
103a5(phal chen): skye dgu nyon mongs me'i gdungs pa dag/sna tshogs thabs kyis rab tu zhi bar byed//'di la gnas pa lus sprul sprin mang ba//ba spu re re'i sgo nasa'agyed byed cing //ngo mtshar rmad kyis phyogs 
103a6(phal chen): kun rgyas bkang nas//chos chu'i char gyis sems can rnam par 'dul//tshul 'jug 'di ni bsam gyis mi khyab gzugs//rgyal sras kun gyi gnas su gyur pa ste//de la gnas shing phyi 
103a7(phal chen): mtha'i bskal par yang //zhing rnams kun tu spyad pa rab tu spyod//bsam pa ji bzhin chos rnams ston byed cing //de dag lhag pa'i dra ba rnam par bzlog/mtho ris mya ngan 'das la sems 
103a8(phal chen): can 'god//thams cad mkhyen pa'i sa yang rnam par ston//'jig rten skya gnas thams cad ma lus bar//kha dog mtha' yas lus mang bsam yas pa//bsam pa bzhin du mos rnams rjod 
103b1(phal chen): byed te//de dag sems can kun gyi gzugs brnyan gzugs//'di dang gzhan dag tshad las 'das pa yi//yon tan rgya mtsho de med rgya mtsho yi//rdul phran rang rnyed bsam gyis mi khyab pa//rnam thar zhi 
103b2(phal chen): ba 'di lan thob par 'gyur//rigs kyi bu kho mos byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa/sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya bas bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du dge ba'i rtsa ba la 
103b3(phal chen): yang dag par bskul ba'i shes par zad na/kho mos byang chub sems dba' 'jig rten thams cad kyi lam gyi rgyud las 'das pa/'jig rten gyi skye gnas thams cad du gzugs 
103b4(phal chen): brnyan rab tu 'byung ba/'jig rten gyi shes pa thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/shes bya'i sgrib pa thams cad kyi ri rnam par 'thor ba la brtson pa/chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin dang 
103b5(phal chen): mtshan nyid rab tu rtogs pa nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa dang /mun pa thams cad rnam par sel ba la brtson pa/chos tham sa cad rab tu dpyod pas mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa/bdag med 
103b6(phal chen): pa'i chos mngon du gyur pa/sems can thams can yongs su smin par bya pa dang /gdul ba rgyun mi gcod pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul gnyis su med par rab tu rtogs pa/dag gi 
103b7(phal chen): lam gyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i blo dang ldan pa/de dag gi spyod pa shes pa 'am/yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug pa 'am/ye shes kyi rnam par gnon pa yongs su shes par 
103b8(phal chen): bya ba 'am/sems kyi gnas khong du chud par bya ba 'am/ting nge 'dzin la dbang sgyur ba rtogs par bya ba 'am/rnam par thar pas rnam par 'phrul pa yongs su shes par bya bar nus re skad 
104a1(phal chen): @#//sor rigs kyi bu 'dzam bu'i gling 'di nyid kyi yul bi ni'i nags tshal na/lum bi ni'i nags tshal gyi lha mo gzi brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'i dpal 
104a2(phal chen): zhes bya ba 'dug gis/de'i gan du song la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su byung ba yin pa dang /ji ltar na 'jig rten rnams la snang ba byed par 'gyur ba 
104a3(phal chen): dang|ji ltar na phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thug par byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa spyad pas mi skyo bar 'gyur ba dris shig gde nas tshod dpon gyi bu nor bzangs gyis mtshan mo'i lha mo 
104a4(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad bsrung ba'i smon lam la brtson pa'i 'od kyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/mtshan mo'i lha mo 'gro ba thams cad bsrung pa'i smon lam la brtson pa'i 'do la 
104a5(phal chen): lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/thal mo sbyar te phyir lta zhing mtshan mo'i lha mo 'gro ba thabs cad bsrung ba'i smon la ma la brtson pa'i 'od kyi drung nas song 
104a6(phal chen): ngo||sangs rgyas rma dag cad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//bam po bdun pa/de nas tshod dpon gyi bu nod bzangs mtshan mo'i lha mo 'gro ba mthas cad bsrung ba'i smon

104a8(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba la yang dag par bskul ba de la yongs su bsgom pa dang /na khong du chud par bya ba dang rgya cher byed cing mthar gyis lum bi ni'i nags tshal gang na bder phyin te/lum bi 
104b1(phal chen): ni'i nags tshal la g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/lum bi ni'i nags tshal na rin po che'i shing gzi brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'i dpal yongs su tshol tshol ba las/lung bi ni'i

104b3(phal chen): seng+ge'i khri rin po che'i pad+mo'i snying po can zhig la 'dug cing /nags tshal gyi lha mo khrag khrig brgya stong phrag nyi shu'i 'khor gyi kun nas bskor te/mdun du byas nas chos bstan to byang chub sems 
104b4(phal chen): dpa' thams cad kyi skye brgya mtshos shin tu bstan pa zhes bya ba'i mdo sde yongs su ston cing de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyud du rigs dang ldan par skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi 
104b5(phal chen): yon tan rgya mtsho'i shugs rnam par 'phel par byed cing 'dug pa mthong nas/lum bi ni'i nags tshal gyi lha mo gzi brdzid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'i dpal gdan ba der song ste phyin 
104b6(phal chen): nas lum bi ni'i nags tshal gyis lha mo gzi brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bar dpos dga' ba'i dpal gyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal nas/mdun du 'dug ste thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad ces 
104b7(phal chen): smras so//'phags ma bdag gi bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed na//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rnam de bzhin gshegs pa'i rig su byung ba lags 
104b8(phal chen): par ma 'tshal/ji ltar na byang chub sems pa'i rnams spyod pa la spyod cing sems can rnams la snang ba 'byin par 'gyur ba yang ma 'tshal to//de skad ces smras pa dang /lung bi ni'i nags tshal gyi 
105a1(phal chen): @#//lha mo gzi brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang po dga' ba'i dpal gyi tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs la 'di skad ces smras so//rigs kyi bu bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' 
105a2(phal chen): rnams kyi skye ba ste/de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skyes pa yin na/de dag las byung na byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams sems kyi skad cig 
105a3(phal chen): re re la'ang byang chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i rtsa bas rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur te/thogs pa med pa dang zhum pa med pa dang phyir ldog pa med pa dang /rgyun gcod pa med pa dang /yongs su skyob 
105a4(phal chen): med pa dang /sgyid lug par 'gyur ba med pa dang /nyams par 'gyur ba med pa dang/nyams dma' bar 'gyur ba med pa dang yongs su yid chad par 'gyur ba med pa dangchud za bar 'gyur 
105a5(phal chen): ba med pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rjes su 'brang bar 'gyur ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul la yongs su gzhol bar 'gyur ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi byang chub tu yongs su smin par 
105a6(phal chen): 'thob par 'gyur ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i sems bskyed pa shin tu yangs par 'gyur ba dang /pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyis rnam par 'phel bar 'byung ba dang /'jig rten gyi lam 
105a7(phal chen): gyi rgyud thams cad las rnam par ldog par 'gyur ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sar yongs su 'grub par 'gyur ba dang /ye shes kyi mngon par shes pa shin tu byang bar 'gyur ba dang /
105a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi chos mngon du 'gyur ba dang /thams cad mkhyen ba'i yul gyi don dang ldan par 'gyur ro/bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sangs rgyas thams cad la bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam 
105b1(phal chen): gyis sbyor ba'i snying po zhes bya ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba dang po'o//byang chub kyi sems kyi sa cha lag yongs su 'grub par 'gyur ba 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba na byang chub sems dpa'i skye 
105b2(phal chen): gnyis pa'o//chos kyi tshul la nges par rtogs par gzhag pa'i sbyor ba'i tshul mngon du gyur pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes byab ni byang chub sems dpa' skye ba gsum pa'o//dus gsum snang bar bya 
105b3(phal chen): ba'i lhag pa'i bsam pa rnam par dag pa'i snying po zhes bya ba ni bya chub sems dpa'i skye ba bzhi pa'o//kun nas snang ba'i 'od kyi snying po zhes bya ba ni byangs chub sems dpa'i skye ba la pa'o//de bzhin 
105b4(phal chen): gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyud du 'byung ba'i snying po zhes byang chub sems dpa'i skyo ba drug pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi stobs kyi 'od snang ba'i rgyan gyi snying po zhes bya ba ni byang
105b5(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i skye ba bdun pa'o//ye shes kyi sgo kun nas rnam par dpyad pas yongs su 'grub pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhesabya ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i skyeb brgyad pa'o//chos kyi dbyings 
105b6(phal chen): sprul pas brgyan par byed pa'i snying po zhes bya ba na byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba dgu pa'o//de bzhin gshegs ba'i sa yongs su gnon pa'i shugs drag po'i snying po zhes bya ba ni byang chub sems
105b7(phal chen): dpa'i skye ba bcu pa'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i skyes pa dang po sangs rgyas thams cad la bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam gyi sbyor ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub 
105b8(phal chen): sems dpa' thog mar sangs rgyas mched cing rim gror bya ba la 'jug ste/de sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams la rim gro dang /bkur sti dang /sti stang dang /mchod pa byed cing bsnyen bkur
106a1(phal chen): @#//byed cing /dgyes par byed la thugs byung bar mi byed pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhal bstabs mi ngoms pa sangs rgyas la rim gro bya bar brtson pas sangs rgyas la dga' ba'i shugs 
106a2(phal chen): kyis rnam par spel ba'i shugs dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa mthong bas mos pa'i shugs drag po yongs su skyes pa/de phyir mi ldog pa'i dad pas bsod nams yongs su bsdud pa'i tshe//de 
106a3(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la mchod pa'i tshogs yongs su bsdu ba la brtson zhing sbyor ba rgyun mi gcod pa yin te/rigs kyi bu de ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi skye ba dang po sangs rgyas 
106a4(phal chen): thams cad la bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam gyi sbyor ba'i snying po zhes bya ba ste/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi tshogs dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su 'grub pa yang dag sdud par 'gyur ba'o//rigs 
106a5(phal chen): kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba gnyis pa/byang chub kyi sems kyi yan lag yongs su grub par 'gyur ba 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang 
106a6(phal chen): chub sems dpa' bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed de/'di ltar sems can thams cad yongs su skyob pa'i phyir snying rje chen po'i sems dang /shin tu dgyes par 
106a7(phal chen): bya ba'i phyir sangs rgyas thams cad bsnyen bkur ba'i sems dang /dngos po thams cad phangs par mi brtsi ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su btsal ba'i sems dang /
106a8(phal chen): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid mngon du 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir brtsams pa chen po'i sems dang /'gro ba thams cad yongs su bsdu ba'i sbyor bas rgyas par dkon pa'i phyir byams pa chen po'i 
106b1(phal chen): sogs dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi go ca brtan par bya ba'i phyir 'gro ba thams cad yongs su mi gtod pa'i sems dang /yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes pa'i snang ba rab tu thob par bya 
106b2(phal chen): ba'i phyir g.yo dang sgyu med pa'i sems dang /byang chub sems dpa'i lam la nan tan du bya ba'i kha 'og tu lus khrus pa'i sems dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi smon lam dang /mi 
106b3(phal chen): 'gal bar bya ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas thams cad la sku bslu ba med pa'i sems dang /phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug par sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba rgyun mi gcod pa'i 
106b4(phal chen): phyir thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du smon lam chen po 'debs pa'i sems dang /de dag la sogs pa byang chub kyi sems kyi yan lag tshogs sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 
106b5(phal chen): yongs su rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skyes pa yin te/rigs kyi bu de ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba gnyis pa/byang chub kyi sems kyi yan 
106b6(phal chen): lag yongs su 'grub par 'gyur ba 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye pa gsum pa chos kyi tshul la nges par gzhag 
106b7(phal chen): pa'i sbyor ba'i tshul mngon du gyur pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes pa ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad kyi tshul rgya mtsho yod do cho ga nges par rtog pas mngon 
106b8(phal chen): du bya ba'i sems dang ldan pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i lam gyi rnam pa yongs su rdzogs par bya bas mngon du bya bar gcig tu sems dpa'i sems dang ldan pa kha na ma tho ba med pa'i las la

107a2(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan thams cad la nan tan du bya bas yongs su grub par bya ba'i sems dang ldang ba byang chub sems dpa'i lam gyi rgyan thams cad mngon par bsgrub pa'i sems 
107a3(phal chen): dang ldan pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du dmigs pa'i tshogs rgya mtsho chen po yongs su bsgrub pa bskal pa'i me 'bar bas kyang /rgyun mi gcod par brtson par bya ba'i sems dang lda 
107a4(phal chen): pa/'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba la rab tu zhugs pas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa kun tu bzang po mngon par bsgrub pa'i sem dang ldan pa/yongs 
107a5(phal chen): su spyod pa'i tshul thams cad la zhugs pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i yon te na yongs su rdzogs par bya ba la dngos po thams cad rnam par bsgom pas dngos po med pa'i tshul la zhugs

107a8(phal chen): 'di la byang chub sems dpa' lhag pa'i bsam pa'i dbyings la gya nom par yongs su dag pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi byang chub la snang ba rab tu thob pa yin byang chub sems dpa' 
107b1(phal chen): tshul rgya mtsho la rab tu zhugs pa yin/bsam pa brtan pa rdo rje'i khams lha dus yongs su zin pa'i sems dang ldan pas shin tu brtan pa yin/srid pa'i lam du rgyud du skye ba las phyir phyogs 
107b2(phal chen): su phyogs pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba yongs su grub par bya ba la mngon du gyur pa yin/byang chub sems dpa'i dbang po rnam par spel ba la khyad par du 
107b3(phal chen): 'bogs pa rab tu thob pa yin/lhag pa'i bsam pa shin tu 'od gsal bar bya ba'i phyir sems shin tu dge ba yin/smon lam brtan zhing rgya che ba rnam par 'phel bar bya ba la mi g.yo ba yin/

107b6(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams kyi byang cub sems dpa'i skye ba lnga pa kun nas snang ba'i 'od kyi snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'gro ba thams cad yongs su

107b8(phal chen): khrims mtha' las pas mtha' las rgal bar rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la bzod pa'i snang ba rab tu thob pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la gnas pa'i

108a4(phal chen): smos pa yin/chos kyi tshul yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du thob par bya ba sa rab tu brtson pa yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba lnga

108b1(phal chen): yin/sangs rgyas kyis byin gyis rlabs yongs su bstan pa'i ting nge 'dzin la brdag nyid che ba'i chos kyi rnam par spyod pa yin/dus la ji ltar 'bab pa bzhin du sems can rnam par dag 
108b2(phal chen): par bya ba'i chos la zhugs pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su dri ba la spobs pa rgyun mi 'chad pa yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'

109a7(phal chen): nge 'dzin la snyoms par 'jug go/tha mi dad pa'i chos rnams la tha dad pa ye shes kyi dbang yongs su ston to//dmigs pa brjod du med pa dag kyang sgrib pa med pa'i dbyings su non par byed do//

109b1(phal chen): rnam par dmigs pa dang mnyam par rab tu rtog par byed do//chos kyi dmigs pa thams cad dang /rnam par shes pa'i rgyud thams cad kyang bsgoms pas khong du chud par byed do//rigs kyi bu 'di ni

109b4(phal chen): thams cad la rgyan sna tshogs dang /rgyan du mar ldan pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing byin gyi rlob ste/sprul pa thams cad la mi 'jigs pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam pa thob pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa 
109b5(phal chen): thams cad la shin tu mkhas par gyur pa yin/chos kyi sprul pa la mi 'jig pa rnam par dag pa yin/chos kyi dbyings dam pa chags pa med pa'i spyod yul dang ldan pa yin/bsam pa ji lta ba

109b7(phal chen): mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongs su ston pa yin/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi lam sgrib pa med pa mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa yin/de'i rgyud kyi chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba la mkhas pa yongs 
109b8(phal chen): ston pa yin/sems can mtha' dang dbus med pa gdul ba'i thabs mngon par bsgrub pa la ma mkhas pa yin/rnam par snang mdzad kyi ye shes kyi dbyig du byas pa'i mdzod dang ldan pas sems can

110a3(phal chen): zhes bya ba gang zhe na/'di la byang chub sems dpa' dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /rang bzhin gcig pa'i yul la dbang bskur ba yin te/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 
110a4(phal chen): mang ba chags pa'i yul la rab tu 'jug pa yin/sems can thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i mtha'i shi 'pho dang /skye ba'i sems rgyun chags par 'byung ba yang rab tu shes so//
110a5(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi ye shes pa dang chags pa'i yul yang rab tu shes so/'das pa dang ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang

110b1(phal chen): 'byang ba thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i drung du nye bar 'gro zhing chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba la mkhas pa'i tshul mdab chags pa yang /sems can gyi khams mtha' dang dbus med pa gdul ba'i thabs

110b3(phal chen): rtogs pa dang /mtshan ma med pa'i mtha'i sgo dang ldan pa'i ye shes mtshan ma thams cad dang bral bas sa bon las myu gu chud mi za ba'i chos nyid dang /rgya las rgya'i gzugs brnyan 'byung ba'i chos nyid dang /gzugs 
110b4(phal chen): las gzugs brnyan 'byung ba'i tshes nyid dang /sgra brnyan dang sgrar mnyam par dmigs pa'i chos nyid dang rnam par dpyod pa dang /rmi lam du mnyam par rnam par dmigs pa'i chos nyed dang /sgyu ma'i rnam pa ltar 
110b5(phal chen): las las longs su byung ba'i chos nyid dang /sems gzugs med pas 'jig rten yongs su bskyed pa'i chos nyid dang /rkyen dang rgyu ji ltar bsags pa bzhin du 'bras bu 'byung ba'i chos nyid dang las ji

110b7(phal chen): ye shes kyi rjes su 'jug pa de lta bus mngon par bsgrub pa'i 'du shes dang /yid la bya bas chod dpon gyi bu nor bzas kyi khang pa brtsegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i snying po'i sgo nas rnam pa 
110b8(phal chen): de lta bur btud de phyag 'tshal nas/yun ring mo zhog lon pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba'i shugs bsam gyis mi khyab pas mngon par brlan pa'i sems dang ldan pa/las dang sems shin tu sim par gyur pa de khang pa

111a2(phal chen): thal mo sbyar te lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/de lta bu'i 'du shes yid la bya bas bskul ba'i sems dang ldan pas tshig tu brjod pa/'di ni srod pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /

111b5(phal chen): dpa' rnams skye bar 'gyur ba'o//'byung bar 'gyur ba'o//yang dag par 'phel bar byed pa'o//rab tu rdzogs par byed pa'o//yongs su grub par byed pa'o//zhing ma lus pa thams cad khong du chud par bya ba'i

112a1(phal chen): @#//kyis mtha' gtugs pa yongs su ston pa'o//sems can tshad med pa spyod pa rgya mtsho sna tshogs dang ldan pa yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul bas yongs su 'dul ba rnams la chos kyi 'khor lo

209b1(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad kyi khongs su chud pa'i lus dang ldan yang/chos kyi dbyings tha dad du bya ba'i gnas la rnam par mi spyod pa dang/gang de dag ma 'ongs pa'i bskal pa thams 
209b2(phal chen): cad du gnas par bya ba'i smon lam dang ldan yang/bskal ba ring ba dang/thung ba'i 'du shes kyi rnam par mi spyod pa dang /gang de dag skra'i rtse mo gcig nas mi g.yo yang 'jig rten

209b4(phal chen): gnas la rnam par spyod pa/mtshan nyid med pa'i gnas la rnam par spyod ba/gnyen po med pa'i gnas la rnam bar spyod pa/mi dmigs pa'i gnas la rnam par

214b7(phal chen): pa mi mnga' ba//sgrib med spyod yul shin tu dgyes pa yi//pho brang 'di la thab sbyar te//bdag kyang lus btud gus par phyag 'tshal lo//rgyal ba'i sras kyi thu bo ste//sgrib med spyod pa mnga' 
214b8(phal chen): ba 'phags pa byams pa de//rnam dag blo mnga' dpe med la//de yi rjes su bsams te phyag 'tshal lo//de nas tshong dang don gyi bu nor bzangs kyis khang pa brtsegs pa chen po rnam par snang mdzad kyis 
215a1(phal chen): @#//brgyan pa'i snying po na 'khod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams la/de dag dang byang chub sems dpa'i bstod pa gzhan tshad med pa dag gis kyang /mngon par bstod 
215a2(phal chen): de shin tu btud nas phyag 'tshal to/rab tu gus par blta ba nas rim gror byas te/mngon du byas nas mchod de khang pa brtsegs pa chen po rnam par snang mdzad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i snying po'i sgo na byang 
215a3(phal chen): chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa la blta bar ni mos/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'i chen po byams pa dang ni phrad par dga' zhing sdod pa las de byang chub sems dpa' byams 
215a4(phal chen): pa khang pa brtsegs pa/de'i phyi rol gyi phyogs gzhan zhig nas srog chags brgya stong du ma'i 'khor dang /lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' 
215a5(phal chen): ldang dang /mi'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po du mas mdun du byas te/g.yas phyogs nas brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten gyi mgon po rnams kyi phyag bya zhing yul na 
215a6(phal chen): gnas pa gnyen bshes su 'ong ba'i bram ze brgya stong mang pos bskor cing mdun du byas te/khang pa brtsegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi rgyun gyis brgyan pa'i snying po logs su 'ong ba mthong nas dga' rangs 
215a7(phal chen): te/mgu zhing nyams bde la tshim zhing yid bde ba skyes nas/byang chub sems dpa' byamasapa gang na ba der logs su mngon du bltas te/thag ring po nas byang chub sems dpa' byems pa la lus thams 
215a8(phal chen): cad kyis phab ste phyag byas so//de nas byang chub sems dpa'i byams pas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs la bltas te/'khor kun la lag pa g.yas pas bstan nas yang dag pa'i yon tan gyis bsngags te 
215b1(phal chen): tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ltos shig bsam pa yongs su dag pa 'di//nor bzangs nor rnams rab tu brtan pa'i bu//byang chub spyod pa dam pa yongs tshol zhing //mkhas pa de'i drung du nye 
215b2(phal chen): bar 'ongs//legs par 'das sam snying rje byams byung khyod//byams pa'i dkyil 'khor yangs pa legs 'ongs sam//legs par 'ongs sam blta na rab zhi ba//spyad pa spyod tshe dub par ma gyur tam//
215b3(phal chen): rnam dag bsam ldan tshur shog 'ongs pa legs//skyon med yid ldan tshur shog 'ongs pa lags//dbang po ma zhum tshur shog 'ongs pa legs //mkhas pa spyod tshe dub pa med grang ngo //chos rnams thams 
215b4(phal chen): cad rnam phye'i don byung ba//'gro ba thams cad rnam gdul rab brtson pa//bshes gnyen thams cad bsnyen bkur rab zhugs pa//brtul zhugs mi g.yo brtan pa khyod 'ongs legs//dge ba'i lam 
215b5(phal chen): gyis 'ongs pa bder 'ongs sam//yon tan lam la gnas pa bder 'ongs sam/rgyal ba'i lam la zhugs pa bder 'ongs sam/lam rnams gang du'ang ngal bar ma gyur tam//yon tan rang 
215b6(phal chen): bzhin tshur shog bder 'ongs sam//dge bas mngon brlan tshur shog bder 'ongs sems//mtha' yas spyod yul legs par bder 'ongs sam//'gro ba kun gyi khyod mthong shin tu dkon//
215b7(phal chen): thob dang ma thob mnyam pa'i yid ldan pa//smad dang sdug bsngal ma grags rnam par spangs//'jig rten chos la ud dpal pad mo bzhin//yid la 'khrul med legs par bder 'ongs sa ma//
215b8(phal chen): g.yo dang sgyu bral bsam pa dge ldan pa//nga rgyal dregs med snod kyi dam pa po//khro dang 'tshig med khengs shing 'gying ba med//blta na sdug pa khyod mthong legs pa 'di//tshur shog phyogs 
216a1(phal chen): @#//kun spyod yul mngon gyur pa//tshur shog rgyal ba'i mdzod rnams bsgrub byed pa//tshur shog rgyal pa'i mdzod rnams 'phel byed pa//yid la dub  
216a2(phal chen): med legs par khyod 'ongs sam//tshur shog dus gsum yul ldan 'ongs pa legs//chos kyi dbyings la mos pa'i dkyil 'khor ldan//sangs rgyas kun gyi yon tan snying por byung//
216a3(phal chen): mkhas pa legs par 'ongs sam mi dal lam//tshur shog 'jam dpal ye shes pa dmo'i mchog/tshur shog sprin dpal char pas rnam spel ba//rgyal ba kun gyi sras pos btang pa tshur//
216a4(phal chen): khyod la sgrib pa med pa'i phyogs kyang bstan//ltos shig smon lam drab ci 'dra ba//chos dbyings rgyas 'gengs bsam gyis mi khyab ste//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod lam lam bsgrubs pa//rgya che yang 
216a5(phal chen): byed nor bzangs 'dir 'ongs so//bde bar gshegs pa'i spyod yul tshol byed cing//rdul dang bral ba'i spyod pa bkrol ba'i phyir//smon lam brlabs chen yongs su 'dri byed cing//skyo ba'i yid med 
216a6(phal chen): 'di ni 'dir 'ongs so//'das pa'i mgon pos gang la bslabs mdzad de//de bzhin ma byon rnams kyang slob 'gyur la//bde gshegs bzhugs pa'i spyod par bya ba rnams//yongs su dris 
216a7(phal chen): phyir 'di ni 'dir 'ongs so/'di ni bdag gis dge bshes chos smras pa//chos kun nan tan yongs su ston byed pa//byang chub sems dpa'ispyod lam yongs ston pa//yin zhes sems gcig 
216a8(phal chen): bsams nas 'dir 'ongs so//byang chub sems dpa' bdag gis blo bskyed pa/sangs rgyas sras po byang chub bskyed byed pa//'di dag bdag bshes sangs rgyas bsngags pa zhes//de ltar 
216b1(phal chen): dge ba'i sems kyis 'dir 'ongs so//'di dag bskyed pas bdag gi ma lta bu//yon tan 'o ma sdud pas ma 'dra//byang chub yan lag yongs su skyong ba yin//dge bshes 'di dag gnod pa rnam 
216b2(phal chen): par bzlog/rga shi mthar byed sman pa lta bu ste//bdud rtsi'i char 'bebs brgya byin lha dbang 'dra//zla ba lta bu'i dkar po'i phyogs kyis rgyas//zhi ba'i phyogs stobs nyi ma gsal ba 'dra//dgra dang 
216b3(phal chen): 'grogs la ri bo 'dra ba ste//rgya mtsho bzhin du mi 'khrug sems dang ldan//yongs su skyong dang mnyan pa 'dra ba zhes//de 'dra'i sems kyis nor bzangs 'dir 'ongs so//'jigs med skyob pas dpa' bo lta bu ste/
216b4(phal chen): ded dpon mgon dang skyabs su gyur pa yin//'di dag bdag la bde stobs dmigs yin zhes//de 'dra'i sems kyi dge bshes bsnyen bkur byed//chos kyi phyogs rnams rtag tu ston pa ste//sangs 
216b5(phal chen): rgyas yon tan ye shes ston pa yin//ngan song ngan 'gro thams cad sbyong bar byed//dge bshes 'di dag bdag la dge ba ston//'di dag rgyal ba'i mdzod kun stob pa yin//'di dag rgyal ba kun gyi mdzod 
216b6(phal chen): glur ste//'di dag rgyal ba kun kyi gsang 'dzin ces//de ltar mkhas pa 'di ni dge bshes snyen//ye shes phun ldan 'di las rnam par bdag/gzugs dang longs spyod cho rigs phun sum tshogs/
216b7(phal chen): 'di la phun sum tshogs kun dkon pa yin//bsam pa de 'dras 'di ni 'dir 'ongs so//bsam brlabs chen 'di la kun kyang ltos//mkhas pa'i dge bshes rnams la 'di ston cing //mkhas pa 'di 
216b8(phal chen): blo'i 'dra bskyed pa ltos//khyod kyis rtag tu de ltar bslab par bya//'di ni sngon gyi mgon dam bdag bde ba'i rgyus//byang chub zhugs te 'jam dpal mthong gyur nas//de yi gdams ngag rjes 
217a1(phal chen): @#//su mthun spyod cing //skyo ba med par rgyu ba 'di la ltos//'di ni dga' ba de thams cad kun spangs te//lha rdzas lta bu'i khyim yang yongs su spangs//pha ma 
217a2(phal chen): ma ma bskyed pa rgya che kun//yongs su spangs shing bran ltar bshes gnyen bsten//mkhas pa 'di ni bsam pa rnam sbyangs te//bdag gis mi lus yongs su spangs byas nas//sangs 
217a3(phal chen): rgyas kun gyi pho brang 'gro bar 'gyur//de phyir 'bras bu de 'dra'ang thob par gyur//'di ni rga dang nad kyis mnar ba yi//srog chags sdug bsngal brgya yis gtses pa rnams//skye shi mya ngan 
217a4(phal chen): 'jigs pas gdungs mthong nas//de dag don du snying rje'i sems kyis spyod//'gro rgyud lnga yi 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor du//sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor btsir ba'i 'gro mthong nas//
217a5(phal chen): sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor 'khor lo 'joms pa yi//ye shes rdo rje sra ba 'di tshol lo//'dod chags zhe sdang rtswa sngong tsher ma can//lha chags rtsa mang yongs su 'dzings pa yi//sems can zhing 
217a6(phal chen): ni yongs su sbyang ba'i phyir//shes rab gshol bstan bzang po 'di tshol lo//gti mug ma rig thibs po'i bsam pa can//'gro ba shes rab mig stor dmigs med pa//de la phyogs bde 
217a7(phal chen): ston pa'i mthu yod pa'i//'gro ba'i ded dpon bzang por 'di 'gyur ro//bzod pa'i go bgos rnam thar bzhon pa zhon//ye shes ral gris mya ngan dgra 'joms pas//mi 'jigs stobs pa'i 
217a8(phal chen): dpa' bor gyur pa 'di//'gro ba rnams kyi lam ston 'gyur ba yin//'di ni chos kyi gru rab sbyor ba yin//ye shes rgya mtsho'i lam la bslabs pa'o//zhi ba'i tshe na mchog gis gling skyel ba//
217b1(phal chen): 'di ni khams gsum mtsho'i skye ba 'dzin//ye shes 'od zer smon lam dkyil 'khor can//sems can kun gyi gnas su snang byed pa'i//chos dbyings mkha' la 'od chen rab ldan par//'di ni sangs 
217b2(phal chen): rgyas nyi mar 'char bar 'gyur//byams pa'i dga' ldan ting 'dzin bsil ba yi//sems can kun la snyoms sems 'od bzang ba//dkar po'i chos kyis dkyil 'khor yongs rgyas pa'i//sangs rgyas 
217b3(phal chen): zla ba 'di ni 'char bar 'gyur//bsam pa'i gzhi rten brtan la rab gnas pa//byang chub spyod pas rim gros 'phags pa ste//chos kun rin chen 'byung gnas ldan pa yi//ye shes rgya mtsho'i mchog tu 'di 
217b4(phal chen): 'gyur ro//byang chub sems kyi glu yi dbang gis phyung //chos kyi dbyings kyi mkha' la mngon par 'phags//dkar po'i 'bras bu'i lo tog 'phel byed pa'i//'di ni 'gro bar chos sprin char 'bebs 'gyur//
217b5(phal chen): dag pa'i snying po'i dri gsum mun sel ba//byams pa'i snum yod dran pa'i snod brtan la//dri med byang chub sems ma 'od bzang po'i//chos kyi mar me 'di ni 'bar byed 'gyur//byang chub sems ni ma ma snying 
217b6(phal chen): rje'i sku//byams pa rdul pa mi g.yo bsam pa 'khrungs//byang chub yan lag mthar gyis 'byung ba yi//sangs rgyas sras su 'di ni yang dag skye//bsod nams snying po mngon par 'phel bar byed//ye shes 
217b7(phal chen): snying po yongs su spyod par 'gyur//'di ni shes pa'i snying po 'char bar byed//smon lam ci bzhin snying po yang dag byung //snying rje byams pas sprul pa 'di 'dra ba//sems can dgrol blo phan pa'i bsam 
217b8(phal chen): ldan pa//rnam dag yid can 'di ni ci 'dra ba//de 'dra'i 'gro ba lha dang mir bcas dkon//'di 'dra'i bsam pa rab tu rtsa brtan la//'di 'dra'i sbyor ba brtan pas rnam 'phel cing //srid gsum g.yog 
218a1(phal chen): par byed pa 'di 'dra ba'i//ye shes shing chen 'bras bu 'byung ba dkon//'di ni yon tan kun grub don du gnyer//chos rnams thams cad yongs su dri bar mos//
218a2(phal chen): the tshom thams cad rnam par gsal don du//le lo med par bshes gnyen thams cad ste//'di ni bdud kyi dkrugs ma nyon mongs 'joms//'di ni lta ba dri ma med par sel//'di ni 'gro 
218a3(phal chen): ba thams cad dgrol ba brtson//mkhas pa 'di ni rtag tu khyad par tshol//yon tan lam la ma nor 'di gnas pas//'di ni ngan song thams cad sbyong bar 'gyur//mtho ris lam 
218a4(phal chen): yang nye bar ston par 'gyur//thar pa'i lam du 'gro ba 'dzud par 'gyur//'di ni 'gro rgyud kun gyi sdug bsngal sel//'di ni 'gro rgyud kun kyi bde ba stob//'di ni srid pa kun gyi zhags 
218a5(phal chen): pa thub//srid pa'i 'gro rgyud thams cad sel bar 'gyur//lta ba dogs pa rnam par 'byed par 'gyur//srid pa'i mkhri shing dra ba gcod par 'gyur//chags pa'i 'dod chags nye bar 
218a6(phal chen): sbyong bar 'gyur//srid gsum lam yang yongs su ston par 'gyur//'di ni 'jig rten mgon dang skyabs yin te//'di ni 'gro ba kun 'od byed pa'o//rnam pa kun tu srid pa sel la mkhas//
218a7(phal chen): sa gsum kun gyi 'dren par 'di 'gyur ro//nyon mongs gnyid log 'gro bsad par byed//'dod pa'i 'dam las mkhas pa 'di sgrol te//'du shes chags las yongs su 'di yang 'grel//
218a8(phal chen): bcings pa kun las thar bar byed par 'gyur//chos dbyings gzhi yi bye brag snang bar byed//'jig rten khams kyi bye brag sbyong bar 'gyur//chos kun gzhi yi bye brag pha rol phyin//nor 
218b1(phal chen): bzangs khyod ni dga' ba bskyed par 'gyur//mkhas pa khyod kyis sbyor ba'i tshul dag dang //dag cing khyod la smad pa med tshul dang //khyod kyi bsam pa yon tan ldan tshul las//bsam pa thams 
218b2(phal chen): cad yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur//khyod kyis bdag nyid dge bar byas tshul gyis//ring por mi thogs sangs rgyas kun mthong 'gyur//ring por mi thogs zhing rnams kun 'gror 'gyur//chos 
218b3(phal chen): kun ring por mi thogs shes par 'gyur//zhing rnams rgya mtsho rnam par spyod par 'gyur//sems can rgya mtsho rnam par 'grel bar 'gyur//spyod pa rgya mtsho yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur //khyod kyi 
218b4(phal chen): yon tan rgya mtsho de 'dra'o//khyod kyi mos pa'i dkyil 'khor tshul las ni//khyod ni yon tan rnams kyi snod du 'gyur//khyod ni dge ba yang dag 'byung gnas 'gyur//khyod ni rgyal ba'i sras dang mnyam par 
218b5(phal chen): 'gyur//khyod kyi smon lam dkyil 'khor tshul las na//khyod kyi bdud kyi dkyil 'khor pham par byed//khyod kyis las kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par sbyangs//nyon mongs dkyil 'khor khyod kyis dag par 
218b6(phal chen): byas//ye shes rgyud ni rnam par spyod par 'gyur //chos kyi rgyud nas rab tu brjod par 'gyur//las dang nyon mongs sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor rgyud//ring po mi thogs rnam par zlog par 'gyur//
218b7(phal chen): 'jig rten 'khor lo srid pa'i 'khor lo brten//shu ba'i las rgyud lnga yi 'khor lor rmongs//'gro kun sdug bsngal 'khor lo gcod pa yi//chos kyi 'khor lo dam pa bskor bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas 
218b8(phal chen): rigs ni rjes su 'dzin par 'gyur//chos kyi rigs ni yongs su spyod par 'gyur//dge 'dun rigs ni yongs su bsdud par 'gyur//rin chen 'byung ba'i 'byung gnas khyod 'gyur ro//srid pa'i drag rnams

219a7(phal chen): skye bo 'phags pa'i lam la 'dul bar 'gyur//brtan pa'i blo gros le lo med pa khyod//mig yangs byang chub lam ni de ltar tshul//srid pa'i rgya mtsho gnas pa'i lus can rnams//sdug bsngal ma mthong
